They block only `backend.search.authorization`. The rest of the module is written and carries its own `ASM-` and `GAP-` entries.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.purpose | status: DERIVED -->\n## Purpose\n\n**Responsible for** the single read operation `API-SEARCH-580505` (GET `/notes/search`). It provides:\n\n- Keyword search over notes (US-003-01).\n- Tag filtering (US-003-02).\n- Keyword and tag filters combined in one request (US-003-04, BR-010).\n- Pagination with a total count (US-003-05, BR-011).\n- Exclusion of archived notes by default, with `include_archived` as the opt-in (US-003-06, BR-009).\n- Pinned-first ordering (US-003-01, BR-019).\n- A search latency of p95 < 300 ms (US-003-03, NFR-006, NFR-007).\n- A 503 response with a `Retry-After` header when the search index is degraded or unavailable (US-003-03).\n\nFor US-006-03 the backend's part is this endpoint. A search that finds nothing returns 200 with an empty array, so the web app can show a \"no results\" message instead of an error. The search box, the message and the form are web-app concerns.\n\n**Not responsible for** (owned elsewhere per the project module map, or by no module):\n\n- Creating, editing, getting, tagging, archiving or unarchiving notes, and the definition of a note, its tags and its archive state. These belong to `notes`. Search only reads them.\n- Listing all notes, or listing archived notes only. No input defines either operation (`GAP-BE-005`, `GAP-BE-006`). Search is not a substitute: it needs `q` or `tags`, and `include_archived=true` adds archived notes to the results instead of replacing the active ones.\n- Login codes, credentials and the access rule. These belong to `auth` and are pending `Q-BE-001`, `Q-BE-002` and `Q-BE-003`.\n- The published OpenAPI document, its example payloads (including the empty-result example, US-004-05) and the spec-versus-route coverage check. These belong to `contract-docs`.\n- Refusal of attachment, real-time and mobile calls. This belongs to `scope-guard`.\n- The residency checks, scans and report. These belong to `residency`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Data model\n\n**Tables owned by this module: none.** Search reads the note and tag-entry structures defined in `backend.notes.data-model`. They are created by migrations 001 and 002 of the project migration plan. They are not restated here (core rule 6). The note fields search relies on are `title`, `body`, `pinned`, the archive state, the timestamps and the note's tags (US-002-01, US-002-06, BR-009, BR-018).\n\n**Objects owned: index artefacts only**, created by migration 003 of the project migration plan (\"Search index on title, body and tags\").\n\n| Index | Definition | Query it serves | Source |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Full-text index over note `title` and `body` | PostgreSQL full-text index, built on an expression over the two columns. It adds no column to the notes table. | The `q` match (BE-SEARCH-FR-001) | US-003-01, `ASM-BE-063` |\n| Tag lookup index, by tag name then note reference | Secondary index on the notes-owned tag entries | The `tags` filter (BE-SEARCH-FR-004, BE-SEARCH-FR-007) | US-003-02, `ASM-BE-063` |\n| Index supporting archive-state and `pinned` ordering | **Not specified.** Added only if the load test fails the p95 target. | Default archived exclusion and pinned-first ordering | NFR-006. No figure is invented. |\n\n**Constraints.**\n\n- The module owns no foreign keys, so there is no `ON DELETE` behaviour to state.\n- The module owns no money columns and no timestamp columns.\n- The full-text index is part of the same PostgreSQL 16 database as the notes (`platform.md`). It is therefore subject to the same EU-region provisioning as the database (BR-001). No external search service is introduced (`ASM-BE-063`).\n- The index is derived from `title` and `body`, so its content follows the note and is removed when the note's data is removed (derived).\n- Encryption at rest of the index is unstated (`GAP-BE-010`).\n- The migration is reversible. Dropping an index loses no data (project `backend.migration-plan`, row 003).\n- Query values are bound parameters. They are never concatenated into query text (NFR-012; BE-SEARCH-FR-023).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## State machine\n\n**Persisted entity states: none.** Search owns no entity with a status column. The note's active or archived state belongs to `notes` (BR-009 filters on `status=active`). A search never changes it (`INV-BE-054`).\n\n**Observed index availability.** Stories describe one externally visible failure mode: \"index degraded or unavailable\" returns 503 (US-003-03). It is modelled as two observed states and is not persisted.\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| AVAILABLE | UNAVAILABLE | An index query fails, or exceeds the request deadline | The deadline value is unstated (`GAP-BE-082`) |\n| UNAVAILABLE | AVAILABLE | A later request's index query succeeds | None |\n\n- **Terminal states:** none.\n- **Per-request evaluation.** Each request is evaluated independently. No circuit breaker or cached health flag is specified (`ASM-BE-070`).\n- **Illegal transitions:** none exist. The only error outcome is 503 with `Retry-After`, with the proposed error code `SEARCH_UNAVAILABLE` from `backend.error-model`.\n- **Triggers.** How \"degraded\" is detected is a gap (`GAP-BE-082`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Requirements\n\nAll requirements implement `API-SEARCH-580505`. Their error statuses and proposed error codes are listed in `backend.error-model`. This section does not restate them.\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-001 \u2014 Keyword match**\n- Given active notes whose title or body contains the keyword,\n- When `GET /notes/search` is called with `q`,\n- Then the response is 200 and contains the matching notes. The match covers `title` and `body` only, not tag names (`ASM-BE-072`). Word matching and case follow `ASM-BE-064`.\n- Traces to: US-003-01, US-006-03, BR-019\n- Verification: (planned) `search_q_matches_title_or_body`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-002 \u2014 No keyword match**\n- Given no note matches the keyword,\n- When the search is executed,\n- Then the response is 200 with an empty array and is not an error.\n- Traces to: US-003-01, US-006-03\n- Verification: (planned) `search_q_no_match_returns_200_empty`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-003 \u2014 No usable filter**\n- Given `q` is supplied but empty, or neither `q` nor `tags` is supplied,\n- When the search request is made,\n- Then the response is 400 with a validation error and no data. A request with `tags` and no `q` is valid (`CON-BE-053`, `Q-BE-058`).\n- Traces to: US-003-01\n- Verification: (planned) `search_without_q_or_tags_returns_400`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-004 \u2014 Tag filter**\n- Given notes tagged `project-x` exist,\n- When `GET /notes/search?tags=project-x` is called,\n- Then only notes carrying that tag are returned.\n- Traces to: US-003-02, US-006-03\n- Verification: (planned) `search_tags_returns_only_tagged`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-005 \u2014 Tag without matches**\n- Given a tag that no note carries,\n- When the search is executed,\n- Then the response is 200 with an empty result set.\n- Traces to: US-003-02\n- Verification: (planned) `search_tags_no_match_returns_200_empty`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-006 \u2014 Invalid tag characters**\n- Given the `tags` parameter contains invalid characters,\n- When the search request is made,\n- Then the response is 400 with error detail. The allowed set is unstated (`GAP-BE-079`, `GAP-BE-008`).\n- Traces to: US-003-02\n- Verification: (planned) `search_tags_invalid_chars_returns_400`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-007 \u2014 Several tags**\n- Given more than one tag is supplied,\n- When the search is executed,\n- Then only notes carrying all supplied tags are returned (`ASM-BE-009`, `Q-BE-004`). The parameter encoding is `ASM-BE-068`.\n- Traces to: US-003-02, BR-010\n- Verification: (planned) `search_multiple_tags_all_of`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-008 \u2014 Combined filters**\n- Given `q=budget` and `tags=finance` are both supplied,\n- When the search is executed,\n- Then only notes matching both are returned.\n- Traces to: US-003-04, BR-010\n- Verification: (planned) `search_q_and_tags_intersection`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-009 \u2014 Combined filters with no overlap**\n- Given the keyword matches but the tag filter excludes every matching note,\n- When the search is executed,\n- Then the response is 200 with an empty result set.\n- Traces to: US-003-04\n- Verification: (planned) `search_combined_no_overlap_returns_empty`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-010 \u2014 Malformed filter combination**\n- Given a malformed filter combination is supplied,\n- When the search request is made,\n- Then the response is 400 with error details. \"Malformed\" is not defined beyond the cases in FR-003, FR-006, FR-014 and FR-018 (`GAP-BE-080`).\n- Traces to: US-003-04\n- Verification: (planned) `search_malformed_combination_returns_400`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-011 \u2014 Ordering**\n- Given matching notes of which some are pinned,\n- When the results are returned,\n- Then:\n  - every pinned note precedes every unpinned note across the whole ordered result set, not only within a page;\n  - within each group, notes are ordered by relevance when `q` is present, otherwise by the proposed order in `ASM-BE-069`;\n  - ties are broken by note id so that pages never overlap or skip.\n- Traces to: US-003-01, BR-019, US-003-05\n- Verification: (planned) `search_pinned_first_stable_across_pages`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-012 \u2014 Pagination and total**\n- Given more matches exist than the page size,\n- When `page` and `limit` are supplied,\n- Then the correct subset is returned together with the total count of all matches, regardless of page.\n- Traces to: US-003-05, BR-011\n- Verification: (planned) `search_pagination_subset_and_total`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-013 \u2014 Page beyond results**\n- Given a page number beyond the available results,\n- When the search is executed,\n- Then the response is 200 with an empty array. The total count still reports all matches (`ASM-BE-073`).\n- Traces to: US-003-05\n- Verification: (planned) `search_page_beyond_returns_200_empty`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-014 \u2014 Invalid paging values**\n- Given `page` or `limit` is negative or non-numeric,\n- When the search request is made,\n- Then the response is 400 with a validation error. The module also rejects `page` = 0 (`ASM-BE-065`) and `limit` above the maximum (`ASM-BE-066`, `Q-BE-059`).\n- Traces to: US-003-05, BR-011\n- Verification: (planned) `search_invalid_paging_returns_400`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-015 \u2014 Default and maximum page size**\n- Given `limit` is omitted,\n- When the search is executed,\n- Then the default page size applies. The module enforces a maximum page size. The proposed values are 20 and 100 (`ASM-BE-011`, `Q-BE-005`, `GAP-BE-009`).\n- Traces to: BR-011, NFR-007\n- Verification: (planned) `search_default_and_max_page_size`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-016 \u2014 Archived excluded by default**\n- Given archived and active notes both match,\n- When a default search is executed,\n- Then only active notes are returned.\n- Traces to: US-003-06, BR-009\n- Verification: (planned) `search_default_excludes_archived`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-017 \u2014 Archived included on request**\n- Given `include_archived=true`,\n- When the search is executed,\n- Then archived notes are returned together with active notes, in the single order of FR-011.\n- Traces to: US-003-06, BR-009\n- Verification: (planned) `search_include_archived_true_adds_archived`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-018 \u2014 Invalid `include_archived`**\n- Given an invalid `include_archived` value,\n- When the search request is made,\n- Then the response is 400 with an error message. Only `true` and `false` are accepted (`ASM-BE-067`).\n- Traces to: US-003-06\n- Verification: (planned) `search_include_archived_invalid_returns_400`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-019 \u2014 Degraded index**\n- Given the search index is degraded or unavailable,\n- When a search is requested,\n- Then the response is 503 with a `Retry-After` header and does not hang. The header value and the deadline are unstated (`GAP-BE-082`).\n- Traces to: US-003-03\n- Verification: (planned) `search_index_failure_returns_503_retry_after`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-020 \u2014 Latency under load**\n- Given a request rate of 50 requests per second,\n- When searches are executed,\n- Then p95 latency is under 300 ms.\n- Traces to: US-003-03, NFR-006\n- Verification: (planned) `search_load_p95_under_300ms_50rps`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-021 \u2014 Large dataset**\n- Given a dataset of about 100k notes,\n- When a search is executed,\n- Then the p95 SLA is still met via indexed lookup.\n- Traces to: US-003-03\n- Verification: (planned) `search_load_100k_notes_p95_under_300ms`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-022 \u2014 Latency at maximum page size**\n- Given a page size of 100,\n- When searches are executed,\n- Then p95 latency stays under 300 ms.\n- Traces to: NFR-007\n- Verification: (planned) `search_limit_100_p95_under_300ms`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-023 \u2014 Injection safety**\n- Given any `q`, `tags`, `page`, `limit` or `include_archived` value,\n- When the request is processed,\n- Then every value is validated and bound as a query parameter. No security scan finds a critical injection vulnerability on this endpoint.\n- Traces to: NFR-012\n- Verification: (planned) `search_injection_payloads_are_inert`\n- API: \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**Story map for this module.**\n\n| Story | Requirements |\n|---|---|\n| US-003-01 | FR-001, FR-002, FR-003, FR-011 |\n| US-003-02 | FR-004, FR-005, FR-006, FR-007 |\n| US-003-03 | FR-019, FR-020, FR-021 |\n| US-003-04 | FR-008, FR-009, FR-010 |\n| US-003-05 | FR-012, FR-013, FR-014, FR-015, FR-022 |\n| US-003-06 | FR-016, FR-017, FR-018 |\n| US-006-03 | FR-001, FR-002, FR-004 (API part only; the UI belongs to web-app) |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-SEARCH-580505` | GET | `/notes/search` | `search` | 200 | 400, 503 | Search notes by keyword, tag, or both combined. Excludes archived notes unless requested. Pinned notes come first. Results are paginated. Returns 503 with Retry-After when the search index is degraded. |\n\n### `API-SEARCH-580505` \u2014 GET `/notes/search`\n\nSearch notes by keyword, tag, or both combined. Excludes archived notes unless requested. Pinned notes come first. Results are paginated. Returns 503 with Retry-After when the search index is degraded.\n\n**Request:** \n- `q` (string): Keyword matched against note title and body. Malformed values return 400 VALIDATION_ERROR.\n- `tags` (array): Tag filter. Combined with q when both are given. A note must carry all supplied tags (ASM-BE-009, pending Q-BE-004). Sent as a query parameter.\n- `page` (integer): Page number for pagination. Invalid values return 400 VALIDATION_ERROR.\n- `limit` (integer): Page size. Default 20 and maximum 100 are assumed (ASM-BE-011, pending Q-BE-005). Invalid values return 400 VALIDATION_ERROR.\n- `include_archived` (boolean): When true, archived notes are included in results. Default false, so archived notes are excluded.\n**Response:** \n- `items` (array, required): Matching notes on the requested page, pinned notes first. Each item shows the note id, title, body, tags, pinned flag, archived state and timestamps.\n- `page` (integer, required): Page number of this result set.\n- `limit` (integer, required): Page size applied to this result set.\n- `total` (integer, required): Total number of notes matching the filters across all pages (proposed; the total count is not stated in the inputs).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.invariants | status: DERIVED -->\n## Invariants\n\n- **`INV-BE-054`** \u2014 No search request creates, modifies or deletes any data. Derived from the read-only nature of the operation and BR-006, BR-007.\n- **`INV-BE-055`** \u2014 Unless `include_archived=true` is supplied, no returned note is archived (BR-009).\n- **`INV-BE-056`** \u2014 In the ordered result set, no unpinned note precedes a pinned note, across pages (BR-019).\n- **`INV-BE-057`** \u2014 Every returned note satisfies every supplied filter (`q`, `tags`, archive rule). No note outside the intersection is returned (BR-010, US-003-04).\n- **`INV-BE-058`** \u2014 The number of items on a page never exceeds the effective `limit`, and the effective `limit` never exceeds the maximum page size. The total count equals the number of matching notes regardless of page (BR-011, US-003-05).\n- **`INV-BE-059`** \u2014 With unchanged data and identical parameters, the order is identical, so consecutive pages neither overlap nor skip (`ASM-BE-069`).\n- **`INV-BE-060`** \u2014 \"No matches\" is always 200 with an empty array, and index failure is always 503. Neither is ever returned as the other, and no 200 carries partial results from a failed index (US-003-01, US-003-03).\n- **`INV-BE-061`** \u2014 A request with an invalid parameter returns 400 and no result data (US-003-01 to US-003-06).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.authorization | status: BLOCKED -->\n## Authorization\n\n**Blocked.** Which role may call `API-SEARCH-580505`, and the ownership predicate that limits which notes a caller sees, depend on project decisions that are still open:\n\n- `Q-BE-001`: who may obtain a login code.\n- `Q-BE-002`: whether notes are owned per user or shared by the team workspace, and the access rule (BR-031).\n- `Q-BE-003`: what credential a verify issues, and whether note and search endpoints require it.\n\n**What would unblock it:**\n\n- Answers to those three questions (see also `GAP-BE-004` and `GAP-BE-085`).\n- Clean source text for BR-030 and BR-031 (`GAP-BE-003`).\n- The contents of the four inbound handoff files (`GAP-BE-001`), if they answer any of these questions.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.jobs | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Jobs\n\n- **Scheduled work:** none is specified or derivable. With the index inside PostgreSQL, it is maintained with the note data (`ASM-BE-063`), so no sync or rebuild job exists. NFR-019 retention does not delete notes, so no sweep touches the index.\n- **Retries:** server-side retries are not specified. A client that receives 503 retries after the `Retry-After` interval (FR-019).\n- **Idempotency keys:** not applicable. The operation is a GET and has no side effects (`INV-BE-054`).\n- **Index health detection:** no input defines a health check or its schedule (`GAP-BE-082`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Test plan\n\n**Unit**\n- Parameter parsing and validation, table-driven: `q`, `tags`, `page`, `limit`, `include_archived` (FR-003, FR-006, FR-010, FR-014, FR-018).\n- The ordering comparator: pinned first, then relevance or the proposed order, then note id (FR-011).\n- Pagination arithmetic: offset, total count, page beyond the last (FR-012, FR-013).\n\n**Integration** (real PostgreSQL 16, per `platform.md`)\n- Seeded active, archived, pinned and tagged notes, covering FR-001, FR-002, FR-004, FR-005, FR-007, FR-008, FR-009, FR-011, FR-016 and FR-017.\n- Failure injection on the index query: a 503 response with `Retry-After` and no hang (FR-019).\n- Page stability: with pinned notes straddling a page boundary, concatenating pages 1..n equals the full ordered set with no duplicates (`INV-BE-056`, `INV-BE-059`).\n- Read-only check: a before-and-after row comparison around a search (`INV-BE-054`).\n- Route-collision check, if the notes module exposes `/notes/{id}`: `/notes/search` must not be handled as a note id. This depends on the notes module path, which is not visible to this module.\n\n**Load and security**\n- 100k seeded notes, 50 requests per second, p95 < 300 ms; also at `limit` = 100 (FR-020, FR-021, FR-022).\n- Injection payloads in `q` and `tags`, plus the security scan (FR-023, NFR-012).\n\n**Contract**\n- The route registry contains `API-SEARCH-580505` and the published OpenAPI document lists it, including its 400 and 503 responses (BR-012; checked by `contract-docs`).\n- Responses validate against the documented schema.\n- `scope-guard` contract tests find no real-time upgrade accepted on this path (BR-015).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.gaps | status: DERIVED -->\n## Gaps, contradictions and assumptions\n\n**Contradictions**\n- **`CON-BE-053`** (non-critical)\n  - Claims: US-003-01 requires 400 when `q` is missing or empty. US-003-02 and BR-010 allow `tags=project-x` with no `q`.\n  - Consequence of each reading: requiring `q` makes tag-only search impossible. Allowing tag-only search relaxes US-003-01's \"missing\".\n  - Reading taken: `q` may be absent when `tags` is present. A supplied-but-empty `q` and a request with neither parameter return 400.\n  - Status: FR-003 and FR-004 are `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED`. See `Q-BE-058`.\n- **Sweep result.** No other contradiction was found in the four directions. No design content was supplied for this module.\n\n**Questions** (degradable; defaults taken because the run is unattended)\n- **`Q-BE-058`**: Is a tags-only search (no `q`) permitted? Default: yes.\n- **`Q-BE-059`**: Is `limit` above the maximum rejected with 400, or clamped? Default: rejected with 400.\n\n**Assumptions**\n- **`ASM-BE-063`**: Search uses PostgreSQL's own full-text index inside the notes database. No external engine is named by any input. Confirm with the platform owner.\n- **`ASM-BE-064`**: `q` is split into words. A note matches when every word appears in its title or body, case-insensitively.\n- **`ASM-BE-065`**: `page` is 1-based, so `page` = 0 is rejected with 400.\n- **`ASM-BE-066`**: `limit` above the maximum is rejected with 400 and not clamped. See `Q-BE-059`.\n- **`ASM-BE-067`**: `include_archived` accepts only `true` and `false`.\n- **`ASM-BE-068`**: `tags` is a single comma-separated parameter.\n- **`ASM-BE-069`**: Within a pinned group, results are ordered by relevance (descending) when `q` is present. Otherwise they are ordered by the note's updated timestamp (descending). Ties are broken by note id. BR-019 says ordering \"is unchanged\", but no prior ordering is defined (`GAP-BE-005`).\n- **`ASM-BE-070`**: An index query failure, or exceeding a request deadline, yields 503. The failure is judged per request, with no circuit breaker.\n- **`ASM-BE-071`**: Each result item uses the same note representation as the get-note response (title, body, tags, archive status, pinned flag), owned by `notes`.\n- **`ASM-BE-072`**: `q` matches `title` and `body` only, and \"content\" in US-003-01 means `body`. Tag names are matched only through `tags`.\n- **`ASM-BE-073`**: A page beyond the available results still reports the full total count.\n\n**Gaps**\n- **`GAP-BE-078`**: Keyword semantics are unstated: word versus substring matching, multi-word handling, case, language and stemming, and how relevance is computed. Closing artefact: acceptance criteria or a decision for the `q` match.\n- **`GAP-BE-079`**: The `tags` encoding for several tags, the allowed characters, and case sensitivity are unstated (related: `GAP-BE-008`). Closing artefact: tag field constraints.\n- **`GAP-BE-080`**: \"Malformed filter combination\" (US-003-04) is not defined. Closing artefact: an acceptance criterion listing the malformed cases.\n- **`GAP-BE-081`**: The behaviour of `limit` above the maximum is unstated. Closing artefact: the answer to `Q-BE-059`.\n- **`GAP-BE-082`**: The definition and detection of \"degraded\", the request deadline and the `Retry-After` value are unstated. Closing artefact: an operations NFR.\n- **`GAP-BE-083`**: The rate-limit class for the endpoint, and any per-caller limit, are unstated. 50 requests per second is a test condition (NFR-006) and not a limit. Closing artefact: an NFR.\n- **`GAP-BE-084`**: The delay between a note change and its appearance in search is unstated. This matters after create, tag and archive operations. Closing artefact: an NFR on search freshness.\n- **`GAP-BE-085`**: Once the access rule is decided, the total count must count only notes the caller may read. Closing artefact: the answers to `Q-BE-002` and `Q-BE-003`.\n- **`GAP-BE-086`**: Search depends on definitions that `notes` must supply: the note representation, the archive-state column, and the tag-entry structure. Closing artefact: `backend.notes.data-model`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-003-01", "US-003-02", "US-003-03", "US-003-04", "US-003-05", "US-003-06", "US-006-03"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/auth-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/auth-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.frontmatter | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Frontmatter\n\n```yaml\nid: auth\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: [Q-BE-001, Q-BE-002, Q-BE-003]\nscope: [API-AUTH-015365, API-AUTH-592570]\n```\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.purpose | status: DERIVED -->\n## Purpose\n\n**Responsible for** the email one-time-code login mechanics (BR-029, BR-032, BR-033; US-006-06; project `backend.module-map`):\n\n- Issuing a 6-digit numeric code (BR-029) with a 10-minute lifetime (BR-020, BR-024, BR-026), delivered by email through AWS SES (`feedback.md`, preflight answer).\n- Limiting code requests to 3 within 10 minutes (BR-028, BR-032).\n- Verifying a code. A wrong, expired or already-used code is refused (BR-023, BR-033). A successful verify consumes the code (BR-023).\n- Locking verification for 15 minutes after 5 wrong codes (BR-022, BR-025, BR-027, BR-032).\n- Retention of challenge, send-attempt and lock records (NFR-019, as read in `ASM-BE-002`).\n- Keeping email addresses and codes out of logs (NFR-018, as read in `ASM-BE-012`).\n\n**Not responsible for:**\n\n- What a successful verify issues to the caller (token type, lifetime, transport). It is undecided: `Q-BE-003`, `GAP-BE-004`.\n- Who may request, verify or receive a code. It is undecided: `Q-BE-001`.\n- Note ownership and the per-note access check. These belong to the `notes` module and are undecided: `Q-BE-002`.\n- The \"existing login methods\" that NFR-016 says remain unchanged. They are not described anywhere: `GAP-BE-012`.\n- Publishing the OpenAPI document. That belongs to `contract-docs`.\n- Out-of-scope refusals. They belong to `scope-guard`.\n- Provisioning SES or any other infrastructure, and the EU-region checks. Those belong to `residency`.\n- The web_app sign-in screens (US-006-06 UI part).\n- The contents of the four inbound handoff files, which were not supplied: `GAP-BE-001`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Data model\n\nNames, types and constraints are designed by this spec. Their only inputs are the entities named in NFR-019 and the rules BR-020 to BR-033. All timestamps are `timestamptz` and stored in UTC. All three tables are in PostgreSQL 16 in an approved EU region (BR-001, `platform.md`). Records are keyed by email address (`ASM-BE-001`, `ASM-BE-114`).\n\n### `login_code_challenges`\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `id` | uuid | NOT NULL | generated | PK |\n| `email` | text | NOT NULL | \u2014 | normalised (`ASM-BE-114`); PII |\n| `code_hash` | text | NOT NULL | \u2014 | never the plaintext code (`ASM-BE-113`) |\n| `issued_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | now | \u2014 |\n| `expires_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | \u2014 | `= issued_at + 10 minutes` (BR-020); CHECK `expires_at > issued_at` |\n| `status` | text | NOT NULL | `'ISSUED'` | CHECK in (`ISSUED`, `USED`, `SUPERSEDED`) |\n| `used_at` | timestamptz | NULL | NULL | CHECK `(status = 'USED') = (used_at IS NOT NULL)` |\n\nThe expired state is not stored. It is derived from `expires_at` (see state machine).\n\n| Index | Serves |\n|---|---|\n| UNIQUE (`email`) WHERE `status = 'ISSUED'` | At most one live challenge per email. Enforces `ASM-BE-115`. |\n| (`email`, `issued_at` DESC) | Verify lookup of the latest challenge for an email |\n| (`issued_at`) | Retention sweep (age 1 day, `ASM-BE-121`) |\n\n### `login_send_attempts`\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `id` | uuid | NOT NULL | generated | PK |\n| `email` | text | NOT NULL | \u2014 | normalised; PII |\n| `requested_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | now | \u2014 |\n| `challenge_id` | uuid | NULL | NULL | FK \u2192 `login_code_challenges(id)` ON DELETE SET NULL |\n\n| Index | Serves |\n|---|---|\n| (`email`, `requested_at`) | Count of accepted requests in the rolling 10-minute window |\n| (`requested_at`) | Retention sweep |\n\n### `account_locks`\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `email` | text | NOT NULL | \u2014 | PK; normalised; PII |\n| `failed_count` | smallint | NOT NULL | 0 | CHECK 0 \u2264 `failed_count` \u2264 5 |\n| `locked_until` | timestamptz | NULL | NULL | CHECK `(failed_count = 5) = (locked_until IS NOT NULL)` is not enforced; see transitions |\n| `updated_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | now | set on every change |\n\n| Index | Serves |\n|---|---|\n| (`updated_at`) | Retention sweep (age 5 days, `ASM-BE-121`) |\n\n**Notes**\n\n- The wrong-code counter lives in `account_locks`. NFR-019 names only \"code challenges\", \"send attempts\" and \"account locks\", so counter storage is `ASM-BE-118`.\n- At-rest treatment of the PII column `email` is unstated: `GAP-BE-010`.\n- The storage scheme for `code_hash` is unspecified: `GAP-BE-134`.\n- Ownership relations to notes are not modelled here (`Q-BE-002`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## State machine\n\n### Challenge (`login_code_challenges.status` plus the clock)\n\n| State | Meaning | Terminal |\n|---|---|---|\n| `ISSUED` and `now < expires_at` | Live. May be verified. | no |\n| `ISSUED` and `now \u2265 expires_at` | **Expired** (derived). A code at or after 10 minutes is expired (`ASM-BE-003`, `Q-BE-006`). | yes |\n| `USED` | Verified successfully once (BR-023) | yes |\n| `SUPERSEDED` | Replaced by a newer accepted request (`ASM-BE-115`) | yes |\n\nA deleted record (retention) is not a state.\n\n| From | Trigger | Guard | To |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| (none) | `API-AUTH-015365` accepted | Fewer than 3 accepted requests in the last 10 minutes | `ISSUED`. Any prior `ISSUED` row for the same email becomes `SUPERSEDED` in the same transaction. |\n| `ISSUED` live | `API-AUTH-592570` with matching code | Account not locked | `USED` |\n| `ISSUED` | clock reaches `expires_at` | \u2014 | Expired (derived) |\n| `ISSUED` | newer request accepted | \u2014 | `SUPERSEDED` |\n\n**Illegal transitions.** A `USED`, expired or `SUPERSEDED` challenge is never re-opened. A submission against one is refused with the codes in the project `backend.error-model` and changes no challenge state.\n\n### Account verification state (per email, `account_locks`)\n\n| State | Condition |\n|---|---|\n| `OPEN` | No row, or `failed_count < 5` and `locked_until` is NULL or in the past |\n| `LOCKED` | `now < locked_until` |\n\n| From | Trigger | Guard | To |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `OPEN` | Wrong code on verify | `failed_count + 1 < 5` | `OPEN`, `failed_count + 1` |\n| `OPEN` | Wrong code on verify | `failed_count + 1 = 5` | `LOCKED`, `locked_until = now + 15 minutes` (BR-022, BR-025) |\n| `LOCKED` | Verify (any code) | `now < locked_until` | `LOCKED`, refused; counter and `locked_until` unchanged (`ASM-BE-118`) |\n| `LOCKED` | clock reaches `locked_until` | \u2014 | `OPEN`, `failed_count = 0` (`ASM-BE-118`, `GAP-BE-013`) |\n| `OPEN` | Successful verify | \u2014 | `OPEN`, `failed_count = 0` (`ASM-BE-118`) |\n\nDuring `LOCKED`, requesting a code is **not** refused (BR-032). There is no terminal state. Rows are removed only by retention.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Requirements\n\nAll requirements follow the project error model (`backend.error-model`): 415 for the login failures (BR-033), with the registry `code` as the discriminator. Request and response shapes are defined in the operation records and are not restated here (core rule 6). Examples that need an email address use a masked one.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-001 \u2014 Issue a code**\n- Given a well-formed email and fewer than 3 accepted requests for it in the last 10 minutes\n- When `API-AUTH-015365` is called\n- Then a 6-digit, all-numeric code is generated (BR-029), a challenge is stored with `expires_at = issued_at + 10 minutes`, a send attempt is recorded, and the code is emailed through AWS SES.\n- Traces to: BR-029, BR-020, BR-032, US-006-06. API: `API-AUTH-015365`.\n- Verification: (planned) `auth.request_code.issues_six_digit_code_and_sends`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-002 \u2014 Resend limit**\n- Given 3 accepted requests for an email within the last 10 minutes\n- When `API-AUTH-015365` is called again for that email\n- Then the call is refused with status 415 and code `LOGIN_CODE_RATE_LIMITED`. No code is sent, no challenge is created, and no send attempt is recorded (`ASM-BE-116`).\n- The window is rolling: attempts with `requested_at > now - 10 minutes`. The limit counts requests including the first (`CON-BE-001` reading).\n- Traces to: BR-021, BR-028, BR-032, US-004-03, US-006-06. API: `API-AUTH-015365`.\n- Verification: (planned) `auth.request_code.fourth_request_in_window_returns_415`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-003 \u2014 Requesting during a lock**\n- Given an email whose account is `LOCKED`\n- When `API-AUTH-015365` is called and the resend limit is not reached\n- Then the request is accepted as in FR-001. Only verification is refused during a lock.\n- Traces to: BR-032. API: `API-AUTH-015365`.\n- Verification: (planned) `auth.request_code.accepted_while_locked`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-004 \u2014 Newest code wins**\n- Given an email with a live `ISSUED` challenge\n- When a new request is accepted\n- Then the earlier challenge becomes `SUPERSEDED` and only the new code verifies (`ASM-BE-115`, `Q-BE-109`).\n- Traces to: BR-032, US-006-06. API: `API-AUTH-015365`.\n- Verification: (planned) `auth.request_code.supersedes_prior_challenge`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-005 \u2014 Successful verify**\n- Given a live `ISSUED` challenge, a matching code, and an account that is not `LOCKED`\n- When `API-AUTH-592570` is called\n- Then the challenge becomes `USED`, `failed_count` resets to 0, and the call succeeds. What the success response carries is **not specified** here (see FR-016).\n- Traces to: BR-023, US-006-06. API: `API-AUTH-592570`.\n- Verification: (planned) `auth.verify_code.success_consumes_code`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-006 \u2014 Single use**\n- Given a challenge in state `USED`\n- When its code is submitted again to `API-AUTH-592570`\n- Then the call is refused with 415 and code `LOGIN_CODE_USED`. No state changes (`ASM-BE-117`).\n- Traces to: BR-023, BR-033. API: `API-AUTH-592570`.\n- Verification: (planned) `auth.verify_code.reuse_returns_415_used`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-007 \u2014 Expiry**\n- Given a challenge with `now \u2265 expires_at`\n- When its code is submitted to `API-AUTH-592570`\n- Then the call is refused with 415 and code `LOGIN_CODE_EXPIRED`. The boundary is inclusive: exactly 10 minutes counts as expired (`ASM-BE-003`).\n- Traces to: BR-020, BR-024, BR-026, BR-033. API: `API-AUTH-592570`.\n- Verification: (planned) `auth.verify_code.expired_at_exactly_10_minutes_returns_415`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-008 \u2014 Wrong code**\n- Given an account that is not `LOCKED` and a well-formed code that matches no usable challenge for the email\n- When `API-AUTH-592570` is called\n- Then the call is refused with 415 and code `LOGIN_CODE_INVALID`, and `failed_count` increments (`ASM-BE-119`). An expired or used code does not increment it (`ASM-BE-117`, `Q-BE-108`).\n- Traces to: BR-022, BR-033. API: `API-AUTH-592570`.\n- Verification: (planned) `auth.verify_code.wrong_code_increments_counter`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-009 \u2014 Lock after 5 wrong codes**\n- Given an account with `failed_count = 4`\n- When a fifth wrong code is submitted\n- Then `locked_until = now + 15 minutes` and the fifth call is itself refused as `LOGIN_CODE_INVALID`. The lock is visible from the next call.\n- Traces to: BR-022, BR-025, BR-027, US-006-06. API: `API-AUTH-592570`.\n- Verification: (planned) `auth.verify_code.fifth_wrong_code_locks_15_minutes`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-010 \u2014 Verification refused while locked**\n- Given an account with `now < locked_until`\n- When `API-AUTH-592570` is called with any code, including a correct one\n- Then the call is refused with 415 and code `ACCOUNT_LOCKED`. No challenge is consumed, and the counter and `locked_until` do not change (`ASM-BE-118`, `ASM-BE-008`).\n- Traces to: BR-022, BR-027, BR-032, BR-033, US-006-06. API: `API-AUTH-592570`.\n- Verification: (planned) `auth.verify_code.locked_account_refuses_correct_code`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-011 \u2014 Input validation**\n- Given a missing or malformed email, or a code that is not 6 numeric digits\n- When either operation is called\n- Then the call is refused with 400 and code `VALIDATION_ERROR`. Nothing is persisted and nothing is counted (`ASM-BE-114`, `ASM-BE-120`).\n- Traces to: BR-029, project `backend.error-model`. API: `API-AUTH-015365`, `API-AUTH-592570`.\n- Verification: (planned) `auth.validation.malformed_inputs_return_400`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-012 \u2014 Atomic, serialised per email**\n- Given concurrent calls for the same email\n- When they are processed\n- Then the resend count, the wrong-code counter and the single-use consumption behave as if the calls ran one at a time (`ASM-BE-122`). Two concurrent correct submissions of one code cannot both succeed.\n- Traces to: BR-023, BR-021, BR-022. API: `API-AUTH-015365`, `API-AUTH-592570`.\n- Verification: (planned) `auth.concurrency.single_use_under_parallel_verify`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-013 \u2014 Delivery through AWS SES**\n- Given an accepted request\n- When the code is sent\n- Then delivery uses AWS SES (`feedback.md`).\n- Whether the send happens in an EU region, and whether the email address leaves the EU, depends on NFR-017, whose text is corrupted: `GAP-BE-135`, `GAP-BE-003`.\n- The response and challenge handling when SES fails are unspecified: `GAP-BE-129`.\n- Traces to: NFR-017, `feedback.md`. API: `API-AUTH-015365`.\n- Verification: (planned) `auth.request_code.sends_through_ses_port`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-014 \u2014 Log hygiene**\n- Given any logging by this module\n- When a log entry is written\n- Then it contains no plaintext code and no unmasked email address (`ASM-BE-012`).\n- Traces to: NFR-018. API: both.\n- Verification: (planned) `auth.logging.no_code_or_raw_email_in_logs`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-015 \u2014 Retention**\n- Given records older than their retention period\n- When the sweep runs (see `backend.auth.jobs`)\n- Then challenges and send attempts older than 1 day and lock rows older than 5 days are deleted (`ASM-BE-002`).\n- Notes are never touched (`INV-BE-001`).\n- Traces to: NFR-019. API: none.\n- Verification: (planned) `auth.retention.deletes_expired_records_only`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-016 \u2014 Credential on success \u2014 BLOCKED**\n- What a successful verify returns and what note and search endpoints require of the caller is undecided. See `Q-BE-003` and `GAP-BE-004`.\n- This requirement cannot be written until they are answered.\n- Traces to: US-006-06. API: `API-AUTH-592570`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-017 \u2014 Eligibility to request or verify \u2014 BLOCKED**\n- Which addresses may request, verify or receive a code is undecided. BR-030 is garbled and the rule is open until decided. See `Q-BE-001`.\n- No eligibility check is specified. As a placeholder only, all well-formed addresses are treated as eligible, which BR-030's own rationale flags as possibly conflicting with the shared-team-workspace default.\n- Traces to: BR-030, US-006-06. API: both.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-AUTH-015365` | POST | `/auth/request-code` | `auth` | 202 | 400, 415 | Request a one-time login code, delivered by email through AWS SES. Limited to 3 requests per 10 minutes; not refused while the account is locked. The code is valid for 10 minutes and single-use. |\n| `API-AUTH-592570` | POST | `/auth/verify-code` | `auth` | 200 | 400, 415 | Verify a one-time login code for an email address. Rejects wrong, expired, already-used codes, and refuses verification while the account is locked (15 minutes after 5 wrong codes). A successful verify consumes the code. |\n\n### `API-AUTH-015365` \u2014 POST `/auth/request-code`\n\nRequest a one-time login code, delivered by email through AWS SES. Limited to 3 requests per 10 minutes; not refused while the account is locked. The code is valid for 10 minutes and single-use.\n\n**Request:** \n- `email` (string, required): Email address the one-time login code is sent to. Must be a valid email address.\n**Response:** \n- `message` (string, required): Human-readable confirmation that a code was sent. Never contains the code.\n- `expires_in_seconds` (integer): Validity window of the issued code in seconds (10 minutes per BR-024).\n\n### `API-AUTH-592570` \u2014 POST `/auth/verify-code`\n\nVerify a one-time login code for an email address. Rejects wrong, expired, already-used codes, and refuses verification while the account is locked (15 minutes after 5 wrong codes). A successful verify consumes the code.\n\n**Request:** \n- `email` (string, required): Email address the code was issued for.\n- `code` (string, required): Six-digit one-time code received by email.\n**Response:** \n- `verified` (boolean, required): True when the code was accepted. The credential type, lifetime and transport issued on success are undecided (Q-BE-003) and are not specified here.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.invariants | status: DERIVED -->\n## Invariants\n\n- `INV-BE-104`: A challenge is accepted by verify at most once. After any verify, a `USED` challenge never returns to another state (BR-023).\n- `INV-BE-105`: No verify succeeds when `now \u2265 expires_at` of the matched challenge (BR-020, `ASM-BE-003`).\n- `INV-BE-106`: No verify succeeds while `now < locked_until` for the email, whatever the code (BR-022, BR-032).\n- `INV-BE-107`: After any operation, at most one `ISSUED` challenge exists per email, and the number of accepted send attempts for an email in any rolling 10-minute window is at most 3 (BR-032, `ASM-BE-115`, `ASM-BE-116`).\n- `INV-BE-108`: No persisted or logged value contains a plaintext code. No log entry contains an unmasked email address (`ASM-BE-113`, `ASM-BE-012`).\n\nCited from the project spec: `INV-BE-001` (retention never deletes a note) and `INV-BE-003` (stored data resides in approved EU regions).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.authorization | status: BLOCKED -->\n## Authorization\n\n**Blocked.** The permission matrix (role \u00d7 resource \u00d7 operation) and the ownership predicate cannot be written. No input defines roles, who may obtain a code (BR-030, `Q-BE-001`), what credential a verify issues (`Q-BE-003`), or how note ownership follows from the caller (BR-031, `Q-BE-002`).\n\nWhat is stated: both operations are the means by which a caller obtains access, so a caller has no credential when calling them. The calls are protected only by the resend limit, the lock and the code rules in `backend.auth.requirements`. The inputs state nothing further about their authentication (US-004-03 mentions 401/403 only conditionally).\n\n**What would unblock it:** answers to `Q-BE-001`, `Q-BE-002` and `Q-BE-003`; or the contents of the four inbound handoff files if they answer them (`GAP-BE-001`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.jobs | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Jobs\n\n**Retention sweep** (NFR-019, `ASM-BE-002`, `ASM-BE-121`)\n\n| Target | Deleted when |\n|---|---|\n| `login_code_challenges` | `issued_at` older than 1 day |\n| `login_send_attempts` | `requested_at` older than 1 day |\n| `account_locks` | `updated_at` older than 5 days |\n\n- Schedule: unspecified (`GAP-BE-016`).\n- The sweep is idempotent: running it twice deletes nothing further.\n- It deletes only rows older than their retention period.\n- The 1-day attempt retention exceeds the 10-minute window, so the sweep never changes a resend-limit decision. A lock lasts 15 minutes against 5 days of retention, so an active lock is never swept.\n- It never touches notes (`INV-BE-001`).\n- It logs only counts, never emails or codes (`INV-BE-108`).\n\n**Retries and idempotency**\n\n- `API-AUTH-015365` is not idempotent. Each accepted call issues a new code and counts toward the limit. No idempotency key is supplied by any input.\n- `API-AUTH-592570` repeated with the same correct code fails the second time as `LOGIN_CODE_USED` (FR-006).\n- Retry of a failed SES send is not specified (`GAP-BE-129`).\n\n**No other scheduled work.** Expiry and lock end are evaluated against the clock at request time, so no sweep job is needed for them.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Test plan\n\n**Unit** (fake clock, in-memory port for SES)\n\n- Code generator: always 6 numeric digits; leading zeros preserved (BR-029).\n- Expiry boundary: 9 min 59 s accepted, exactly 10 min refused as expired, after 10 min refused as expired (FR-007).\n- Resend window: three accepted, fourth refused; the oldest attempt falling out of the window frees a slot (FR-002).\n- Lock: counter 0\u21925, the fifth wrong code locks for 15 minutes, a correct code during the lock is refused (FR-008 to FR-010).\n- Counter resets: successful verify and lock expiry both reset to 0 (`ASM-BE-118`).\n- Log redaction: no plaintext code or raw email in any emitted line (FR-014).\n\n**Integration** (PostgreSQL 16)\n\n- Full flow: request \u2192 verify success \u2192 reuse refused (FR-001, FR-005, FR-006).\n- Supersede: a second request makes the first code unusable (FR-004); the partial unique index rejects two live challenges for one email.\n- Parallel verify of one correct code: exactly one success (FR-012). Parallel requests at the limit: at most 3 accepted.\n- Request during lock accepted; verify during lock refused (FR-003, FR-010).\n- Retention: seeded old and new rows; only old rows are deleted; notes untouched (FR-015).\n- SES failure path, once `GAP-BE-129` is answered.\n\n**Contract**\n\n- The published OpenAPI document lists both operations (US-004-01). Each documents 400, the 415 cases with their registry codes, and a locked-account response (US-004-03). Examples use masked emails (NFR-009, project core rule).\n- Contract tests assert that the module exposes no endpoint beyond the two operations (`scope-guard`, NFR-010).\n- Because 415 is overloaded (project `backend.error-model`), contract tests assert the `code` value for each case, not the status alone.\n- Tests for FR-016 and FR-017 are not written until `Q-BE-001` and `Q-BE-003` are answered.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.gaps | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Gaps, contradictions, assumptions and questions\n\n### Contradictions\n\nNo new `CON-` is minted by this module. `CON-BE-001` (BR-021 \"resend\" versus BR-028/BR-032 \"requests\") is in force and read as in FR-002. The reading of BR-027's \"login attempts\" is `ASM-BE-008`.\n\n### Questions (all degradable; defaults taken because the run is unattended)\n\n- `Q-BE-108` (degradable): Do expired-code and used-code submissions count toward the 5 wrong codes? Default: no (`ASM-BE-117`).\n- `Q-BE-109` (degradable): Does a new accepted request invalidate the earlier live code? Default: yes (`ASM-BE-115`).\n- `Q-BE-110` (degradable): Does the wrong-code counter reset on successful verify and on lock expiry, and does it have a time window? Default: resets on both; no window (`ASM-BE-118`).\n\nBlocking questions already open in the project spec and relied on here: `Q-BE-001`, `Q-BE-002`, `Q-BE-003`. Degradable `Q-BE-006` (expiry boundary) is applied via `ASM-BE-003`.\n\n### Assumptions\n\n- `ASM-BE-113`: Codes are generated with a cryptographically secure random source and stored only as a hash (`code_hash`), never in plaintext. Confirm with a platform security input (`GAP-BE-134`).\n- `ASM-BE-114`: Email is trimmed and lowercased before use as a key. A value that is not a syntactically valid email address is a 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR`. Confirm with `GAP-BE-128`.\n- `ASM-BE-115`: An accepted request supersedes any earlier `ISSUED` challenge for that email. Confirm with `Q-BE-109`.\n- `ASM-BE-116`: A request refused by the resend limit is not recorded as a send attempt and does not extend the window. The window is rolling.\n- `ASM-BE-117`: Submissions of an expired or used code do not increment `failed_count`. Confirm with `Q-BE-108`.\n- `ASM-BE-118`: `failed_count` lives in `account_locks`. It resets to 0 on successful verify and on lock expiry. Refused verifications during a lock change nothing, and the lock is not extended. Confirm with `Q-BE-110` and `GAP-BE-013`.\n- `ASM-BE-119`: A well-formed code that matches no usable challenge, including an email with no challenge at all, is `LOGIN_CODE_INVALID` and counts as a wrong code. Confirm with `GAP-BE-133`.\n- `ASM-BE-120`: A code that is not exactly 6 numeric digits is a 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` and is not counted as a wrong code.\n- `ASM-BE-121`: Retention age is measured from `issued_at` (challenges), `requested_at` (attempts) and `updated_at` (locks).\n- `ASM-BE-122`: Operations for the same email are serialised (row locking or equivalent). The mechanism is an implementation choice.\n\n### Gaps\n\nEach names the artefact that would close it.\n\n- `GAP-BE-128`: Email normalisation, maximum length and format rules are unstated. Closing artefact: field constraints for the login endpoints.\n- `GAP-BE-129`: The response and the challenge's fate when SES fails to send are unspecified, as is retry. Default taken: the challenge is voided and the status is unspecified. Closing artefact: an acceptance criterion for delivery failure.\n- `GAP-BE-130`: The success status codes of the two operations are not supplied by any input. Closing artefact: acceptance criteria stating them.\n- `GAP-BE-131`: Rate-limit class beyond the stated 3 per 10 minutes is unspecified, including whether limits also apply per client address. Closing artefact: an NFR.\n- `GAP-BE-132`: Whether the request-code response may reveal that an address is unknown or ineligible is unspecified. Closing artefact: the answer to `Q-BE-001`.\n- `GAP-BE-133`: Verify behaviour for an email that never requested a code (`ASM-BE-119`) is unstated. Closing artefact: an acceptance criterion.\n- `GAP-BE-134`: The hashing scheme and any pepper or key management for `code_hash` are unspecified. Closing artefact: a platform security input.\n- `GAP-BE-135`: The SES region, sender identity and whether the email address leaves the EU are undetermined. Closing artefact: the region list (`GAP-BE-002`) and clean NFR-017 text (`GAP-BE-003`).\n\nAlready open in the project spec and relied on: `GAP-BE-001` (inbound handoffs unread), `GAP-BE-003` (garbled source text), `GAP-BE-004` (credential after verify), `GAP-BE-010` (encryption at rest), `GAP-BE-012` (existing login methods), `GAP-BE-013` (counter reset), `GAP-BE-016` (sweep schedule).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-006-06"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/contract-docs-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/contract-docs-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.frontmatter | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Frontmatter\n\n```yaml\nid: contract-docs\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: []\nscope: [API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307]\n```\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.purpose | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Purpose\n\n**Responsible for** (source: `backend.module-map`; EP-004, EP-005):\n\n- Publishing one OpenAPI 3.x document that covers every implemented `notes_api` endpoint, with request and response schemas, 4xx/5xx errors, example payloads, a data-residency handling note and a Scope section (US-004-01 to US-004-06, US-005-01; BR-012, BR-013; NFR-008, NFR-009).\n- Serving that document through `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n- A spec-versus-route coverage check, which reports the percentage of implemented endpoints present in the document and names any that are missing (US-004-04; BR-012; NFR-008).\n- A spec-validation check that fails when documented types diverge from code (US-004-02).\n- A documentation lint or build step that fails when the Scope section is missing (US-005-01).\n- Recording the interface changes that consumers must adapt to: `pinned` and pinned-first ordering, and the email one-time-code login endpoints (NFR-014, NFR-015, NFR-016).\n\n**Not responsible for:**\n\n- The behaviour, data model or error semantics of any documented endpoint. These are owned by `notes`, `search` and `auth`. This module documents them and references `backend.error-model` and the other modules' contracts; it restates none of them (core rule 6).\n- Detecting or refusing attachment, real-time and mobile-specific calls. That belongs to `scope-guard` (BR-014 to BR-016, NFR-010, NFR-011). This module only states the exclusions in the Scope section.\n- Logging and classifying stakeholder requests (BR-017, US-005-06). See `GAP-BE-019` in `backend.coverage`.\n- The residency infrastructure, scans and compliance report (`residency` module).\n- Versioning, tagging or publishing the contract package. The orchestrator owns these (core \u00a72, backend B3).\n- Defining `API-` or `SCHEMA-` ids. DevAgent mints them.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Data model\n\n**No tables, columns or indexes.** This module persists no data (`ASM-BE-171`). The OpenAPI document is a build artefact generated from the route registry and shipped with the deployment (`ASM-BE-167`). No input specifies storage for it.\n\n**Logical outputs** (not persisted, `ASM-BE-170`): each CI run of the coverage check produces a report containing:\n\n| Field | Meaning | Nullability | Source |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| implemented endpoint count | number of (method, path) pairs in the route registry | not null | US-004-04, BR-012 |\n| documented endpoint count | number of those pairs present in the document | not null | US-004-04 |\n| coverage percentage | documented \u00f7 implemented (`ASM-BE-168`) | null when the document cannot be parsed | US-004-04 |\n| missing endpoints | list of (method, path) pairs not documented | empty when coverage is 100% | US-004-04 |\n| parse error | description of why the document could not be read | not null only on parse failure | US-004-04 |\n\nThe report format and retention are unstated (`GAP-BE-181`).\n\n**Tables belonging to other modules** (notes, tags, login challenges, attempts and locks) are described by those modules and by `backend.domain-model`. They are not repeated here.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## State machine\n\nThe entity with state is the generated document, per build. No input defines this machine; it is derived from the acceptance criteria listed in each row.\n\n| State | Meaning |\n|---|---|\n| `GENERATED` | Document produced from the route registry |\n| `VALIDATED` | Passed spec validation, the coverage check at 100%, and the Scope-section lint |\n| `REJECTED` | A check failed; the build fails (**terminal** for that build) |\n| `PUBLISHED` | Shipped and served by `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`; superseded only by the next build's document |\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Source |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `GENERATED` | `VALIDATED` | CI build runs all checks | document parses; every documented type matches code; coverage is 100%; Scope section present | US-004-02, US-004-04, US-005-01 |\n| `GENERATED` | `REJECTED` | CI check fails | any guard above is false | US-004-02, US-004-04, US-005-01 |\n| `VALIDATED` | `PUBLISHED` | deployment | new endpoints appear in the document before or at the same time they become callable | US-004-01 |\n\n**Coverage-check outcomes** (all inside `GENERATED`):\n\n| Outcome | Condition | Result |\n|---|---|---|\n| Pass | every implemented endpoint documented | reports 100% |\n| Below 100% | at least one implemented endpoint is undocumented | reports less than 100% and names it |\n| Parse error | the document is malformed | fails with a clear parsing error and never reports a coverage percentage |\n\n**Terminal states:** `REJECTED`, and `PUBLISHED` once superseded. Publishing a document that skipped `VALIDATED` is not permitted. No HTTP status applies, because the transition is a pipeline decision, not a request (`ASM-BE-169`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.requirements | status: UNKNOWN -->\n## Requirements\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-001: Serve the published document**\n- Given `notes_api` is deployed, when a client requests `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`, then a valid OpenAPI 3.x document is returned.\n- The document lists the CRUD, tagging, search, archive and email one-time-code login (send and verify) endpoints.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01 (AC 1); BR-012; NFR-008.\n- **Verification.** `contract_docs.serves_valid_openapi_3x` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-002: Document every implemented endpoint, including this one**\n- Given the route registry, when the document is generated, then every implemented endpoint has an entry, including `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307` itself (`ASM-BE-166`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01; BR-012; NFR-008.\n- **Verification.** `contract_docs.every_route_has_entry` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-003: New endpoints are documented no later than they are callable**\n- Given a new endpoint is added, when the document is regenerated and published, then the endpoint appears in it before or at the same time it becomes callable.\n- It is generated from the route registry and shipped in the same deployment (`ASM-BE-167`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01 (AC 2).\n- **Verification.** `contract_docs.new_route_documented_on_deploy` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-004: Content negotiation**\n- Given a request with an `Accept` header, when the header names a supported representation (JSON or YAML, `ASM-BE-164`), then the document is returned in that representation.\n- Given no preference is stated, the response is JSON.\n- Given an unsupported `Accept` value, the response is 406 with code `NOT_ACCEPTABLE` (`ASM-BE-163`, `Q-BE-158`).\n- The response states its content type clearly in every case.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01 (AC 3); `backend.error-model`.\n- **Verification.** `contract_docs.accept_negotiation` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-005: Schemas carry required fields, types and constraints**\n- Given an endpoint accepts a note payload, when its schema is inspected in the document, then all required fields, types and constraints are documented.\n- Constraints are limited to those supplied; title length, body size and tag format are not (`GAP-BE-008`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-02 (AC 1); BR-012.\n- **Verification.** `contract_docs.schemas_have_required_and_types` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-006: Optional parameters are marked with defaults**\n- Given an endpoint has optional query parameters (for example search filters), when the schema is reviewed, then each is marked optional and its default value is documented.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-02 (AC 2).\n- **Verification.** `contract_docs.optional_params_have_defaults` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-007: Type drift fails CI**\n- Given a documented field type is changed in code and the document is not updated, when the spec-validation check runs, then the CI check fails and flags the mismatch.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-02 (AC 3).\n- **Verification.** `contract_docs.type_drift_fails_ci` (planned).\n- **API.** None.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-008: Documented error responses**\n- Given an endpoint, the document includes its 4xx and 5xx responses, and includes the following:\n  - (a) A 400 response with the error structure for any endpoint that can return validation errors.\n  - (b) 401 and 403 with their body shape for any endpoint that requires authentication or authorisation. Applicability depends on `Q-BE-003`.\n  - (c) For the request-code and verify-code endpoints, a 415 response for more than 3 requests within 10 minutes, and a locked-account response after 5 wrong codes, locked for 15 minutes.\n  - (d) A 404 response that matches actual behaviour (`CON-BE-153`).\n- The error envelope and code registry are referenced from `backend.error-model`, not restated.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-03 (AC 1 to 3); BR-012; NFR-016.\n- **Verification.** `contract_docs.error_responses_documented_and_match` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-009: Coverage metric**\n- Given the coverage check runs, when all implemented endpoints are documented, then it reports 100%.\n- Given an implemented endpoint is undocumented, then it reports less than 100% and names the missing endpoint.\n- Given the document is malformed, then it fails with a parsing error and does not report coverage.\n- The metric is exposed as the CI job's report (`ASM-BE-170`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-04 (AC 1 to 3); NFR-008.\n- **Verification.** `contract_docs.coverage_full`, `contract_docs.coverage_names_missing`, `contract_docs.coverage_parse_error` (planned).\n- **API.** None.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-010: Examples**\n- Given the search and tagging operations, when the document is viewed, then each has an example request (with query parameters where applicable) and an example response body.\n- An example empty-result response is included for search.\n- Examples contain no credentials or real-looking PII (core \u00a712).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-05; NFR-009.\n- **Verification.** `contract_docs.search_and_tagging_examples_present` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-011: Data-handling note**\n- Given the document, when an operations engineer reviews it, then a data-handling note states that all note data is stored and served within the EU.\n- The note calls out any difference between archive and active endpoints in storage location. The inputs show none: archive is a flag on the same record (BR-007; `backend.data-lifecycle`). The note states there is no difference.\n- The note names no specific region (`GAP-BE-002` in `backend.coverage`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-06 (AC 1, 2); BR-013; BR-001.\n- **Verification.** `contract_docs.data_handling_note_present` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-012: Scope section**\n- Given the published API documentation, when a reader views the Scope section, then mobile apps, attachments and real-time collaboration are listed as explicitly excluded (`ASM-BE-172`).\n- **Traces to.** US-005-01 (AC 1); BR-014 to BR-016.\n- **Verification.** `contract_docs.scope_section_lists_exclusions` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-013: Scope changes need review; missing Scope fails the build**\n- Given a proposed documentation change that adds or changes an out-of-scope item, then it requires review and approval before merge. The mechanism is unstated (`GAP-BE-179`).\n- Given a docs build in which the Scope section is missing, then the build fails with a lint or validation error.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-01 (AC 2, 3).\n- **Verification.** `contract_docs.missing_scope_fails_build` (planned); the review gate is process-level.\n- **API.** None.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-014: Interface-change notices**\n- Given the interface changes `pinned` with pinned-first ordering, and email one-time-code login as additive with existing login methods unchanged, then the document records each as an interface change that consumers must adapt to.\n- How consumers are notified is unstated (`GAP-BE-180`).\n- **Traces to.** NFR-014, NFR-015, NFR-016; US-006-06 (consumer).\n- **Verification.** `contract_docs.interface_change_notes_present` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307` | GET | `/openapi` | `contract-docs` | 200 | 406 | Retrieve the published OpenAPI 3.x document covering every implemented endpoint, including schemas, 4xx/5xx errors, examples, the data-residency handling note and the Scope section. |\n\n### `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307` \u2014 GET `/openapi`\n\nRetrieve the published OpenAPI 3.x document covering every implemented endpoint, including schemas, 4xx/5xx errors, examples, the data-residency handling note and the Scope section.\n\n**Request:** \n- `Accept` (string): Content-negotiation header (not a body or query field) selecting the document format. An unsupported value yields 406 or a default-format fallback with a clear content type (US-004-01).\n**Response:** \n- `openapi` (string, required): OpenAPI 3.x version string of the published document.\n- `info` (object, required): Document metadata, including the Scope section (in-scope and excluded capabilities: attachments, real-time, mobile) and the data-residency handling note.\n- `paths` (object, required): Every implemented endpoint with parameters, request bodies, success responses, 4xx/5xx error responses and examples; 100% of implemented endpoints must be present (NFR-008).\n- `components` (object, required): Reusable schemas, including the error envelope, referenced by the paths.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.invariants | status: DERIVED -->\n## Invariants\n\n- `INV-BE-154`: After every build that reaches `PUBLISHED`, every implemented endpoint appears in the document (BR-012, NFR-008).\n- `INV-BE-155`: The published document is valid OpenAPI 3.x (US-004-01).\n- `INV-BE-156`: The published document always contains the Scope section and the data-handling note (US-005-01, BR-013, US-004-06).\n- `INV-BE-157`: Documented response statuses and field types match actual behaviour; a mismatch fails CI (US-004-02, US-004-03).\n- `INV-BE-158`: The coverage check never reports a percentage for a document it could not parse (US-004-04).\n- `INV-BE-159`: No attachment, real-time or mobile-specific endpoint is documented as available. The Scope section lists them only as excluded (BR-014 to BR-016, NFR-010).\n- `INV-BE-160`: No example in the document contains a secret or real-looking PII (core \u00a712).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.authorization | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Authorization\n\n| Operation | Roles permitted | Source |\n|---|---|---|\n| `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307` | Any caller, with no credential (`ASM-BE-165`, `Q-BE-159`) | `ASM-BE-165` |\n\n**Ownership predicate.** None. The document is not user-scoped.\n\n**Dependency on blocked decisions.** `backend.auth-model` is `BLOCKED` pending `Q-BE-001`, `Q-BE-002` and `Q-BE-003`. If a credential is later required for all endpoints, this section changes. Nothing else in the module depends on the answer, except the 401/403 documentation in FR-008(b).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.jobs | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Jobs\n\nThere is no scheduled work and no retries. All jobs are CI-triggered (`ASM-BE-169`).\n\n| Job | Trigger | Effect | Source |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Generate document | each build | produces the document from the route registry (`ASM-BE-167`) | US-004-01 |\n| Spec validation | each build | fails on documented-versus-code type mismatch | US-004-02 |\n| Coverage check | each build | reports coverage; fails below 100% or on parse error | US-004-04 |\n| Scope-section lint | each docs build | fails if the Scope section is missing | US-005-01 |\n\n**Idempotency.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307` is a GET and is idempotent. The CI jobs are pure functions of the code and document, so they need no idempotency keys.\n\nThe CI system, generation tool and mismatch-detection mechanism are unnamed (`GAP-BE-178`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Test plan\n\n**Unit**\n- Coverage computation: 100%, below 100% naming the missing pair, and parse error (FR-009).\n- Content negotiation: JSON, YAML, none, and unsupported (FR-004).\n- Scope-section lint: present and absent (FR-013).\n\n**Integration**\n- `GET` on `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307` returns a document that parses as OpenAPI 3.x (FR-001).\n- A route added in a test build appears in the document (FR-003).\n- Every route in the registry has an entry, including this endpoint (FR-002).\n- A type changed in code without a document update fails spec validation (FR-007).\n\n**Contract**\n- Documented 400, 404 and 415 responses match actual behaviour (FR-008).\n- Search and tagging operations have examples, including the empty search result (FR-010).\n- The data-handling note and Scope section are present (FR-011, FR-012).\n- No documented path matches an attachment, real-time or mobile pattern (`INV-BE-159`). The enforcement test belongs to `scope-guard`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.gaps | status: DERIVED -->\n## Gaps, contradictions, assumptions and questions\n\n**Contradiction (non-critical)**\n- `CON-BE-153`: US-004-03 AC 3 pairs the precondition \"a documented field type is changed in code\" with the check \"the 404 response matches the spec\". The precondition and the check concern different things.\n  - This spec reads the criterion as: the documented 404 response must match actual API behaviour (FR-008(d)).\n  - The type-change precondition is covered separately by FR-007.\n\n**Questions (degradable, default taken)**\n- `Q-BE-158`: For an unsupported `Accept`, return 406 or fall back to a default format? Default: 406 (`ASM-BE-163`).\n- `Q-BE-159`: Should `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307` require a credential? Default: no (`ASM-BE-165`). This is related to the blocking `Q-BE-003`.\n\n**Assumptions**\n- `ASM-BE-163`: An unsupported `Accept` returns 406 with code `NOT_ACCEPTABLE`, as in `backend.error-model`.\n- `ASM-BE-164`: JSON and YAML are both served, and JSON is returned when no preference is stated.\n- `ASM-BE-165`: `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307` needs no credential.\n- `ASM-BE-166`: The endpoint documents itself.\n- `ASM-BE-167`: The document is generated from the route registry at build time and shipped in the same deployment as the code.\n- `ASM-BE-168`: The coverage unit is a (method, path) pair, and coverage is documented \u00f7 implemented.\n- `ASM-BE-169`: Checks run on every CI build, in parallel with NFR-010's per-build contract test.\n- `ASM-BE-170`: The coverage metric is exposed as the CI job report, not as a runtime endpoint. Only one operation is minted for this module.\n- `ASM-BE-171`: This module has no persistent storage.\n- `ASM-BE-172`: The Scope section lists exactly the three exclusions in US-005-01.\n\n**Gaps** (each names its closing artefact)\n- `GAP-BE-178`: The OpenAPI generation tool, CI system, and the mechanism that detects documented-versus-code type mismatch are unnamed. Closing artefact: a `platform.md` addition (see also `GAP-BE-011` in `backend.coverage`).\n- `GAP-BE-179`: The review and approval mechanism for Scope-section changes (approver role, enforcement) is unstated. Closing artefact: a process or repository-policy input.\n- `GAP-BE-180`: How consumers are notified of interface changes (NFR-014 to NFR-016) is unstated: channel, audience and timing. Closing artefact: an NFR or process input.\n- `GAP-BE-181`: The coverage-report format and retention, and the rate-limit class and caching behaviour of `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`, are unstated. Closing artefact: an NFR.\n- `GAP-BE-182`: The 5xx behaviour when the document is unavailable is unstated. Closing artefact: an acceptance criterion.\n- `GAP-BE-183`: The relationship between the served document, the machine-readable `contracts/openapi.yaml` that the orchestrator publishes, and the document version is unstated. Closing artefact: an orchestrator or platform input.\n\n**Referenced, not re-minted:** `GAP-BE-001` (the four inbound handoff files, not supplied), `GAP-BE-002`, `GAP-BE-003`, `GAP-BE-008` and `GAP-BE-011`, all in `backend.coverage`. `Q-BE-001`, `Q-BE-002` and `Q-BE-003` are blocking and sit in the project spec.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-004-01", "US-004-02", "US-004-03", "US-004-04", "US-004-05", "US-004-06", "US-005-01"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/scope-guard-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/scope-guard-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guard.frontmatter | status: DERIVED -->\n## Frontmatter\n\n```yaml\nid: scope-guard\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: []\nscope: []\n```\n\n`scope` is empty because the operation list supplied for this module contained no operation records, so DevAgent minted no `API-` ids for it (`GAP-BE-229`). The module map defines `scope-guard` as request middleware applied to the whole HTTP surface (`backend.module-map`). It may therefore own no endpoint of its own. No `API-` id is written in this document.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guard.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guard.purpose | status: DERIVED -->\n## Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** `scope-guard` enforces the documented scope boundary of `notes_api`. It does two things (EP-005; BR-014, BR-015, BR-016; NFR-010, NFR-011):\n\n1. **Runtime refusal.** It refuses requests that belong to the excluded categories, which are attachment or file upload, real-time channels, and mobile-specific endpoints. It answers each with a structured `OUT_OF_SCOPE` error, not a silent failure or a bare 404 (US-005-02, US-005-03, US-005-04, BR-016).\n2. **Build-time proof.** It supplies the automated contract tests that prove no excluded endpoint exists in the OpenAPI spec or the route registry. These tests run on every CI build and block a merge on a match (US-005-05, BR-014, BR-015, NFR-010).\n\n**Not responsible for:**\n\n- The Scope section of the published API documentation, its review gate and its docs-build lint (US-005-01). That belongs to `contract-docs`.\n- Logging and classifying stakeholder requests for mobile, attachments or real-time collaboration (US-005-06, BR-017). This is a product-manager process (`GAP-BE-019`).\n- Defining the error envelope or the registry of codes. `backend.error-model` owns them; this module only uses `OUT_OF_SCOPE`.\n- Note create, edit and PATCH semantics, including concurrent-edit handling (US-005-03, second criterion). The `notes` module owns that behaviour (`ASM-BE-005`); this module only requires that no live broadcast or merge logic exists.\n- Residency checks, authentication, login rate limiting and search behaviour.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guard.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guard.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Data model\n\n**There are no tables, columns or indexes.** No input requires `scope-guard` to persist anything, and the module map describes it as middleware with no module dependencies (`ASM-BE-213`). It adds no migration to `backend.migration-plan`.\n\n**Configuration (not persisted):** an exclusion registry held in code, with these logical attributes:\n\n| Attribute | Type | Meaning | Source |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `category` | enum: `attachment`, `real-time`, `mobile` | The excluded category | BR-014, BR-015, BR-016 |\n| `matcher` | path pattern, header predicate or media-type predicate | How a request or route is recognised | US-005-02, US-005-03, US-005-05 |\n| `reason` | string | The human-readable reason returned in the refusal | US-005-04 |\n\n**Seed entries.** Only the examples named in the inputs are seeded (`ASM-BE-214`):\n\n- `attachment`: a path ending in an `attachments` segment, for example `POST /notes/{id}/attachments` (US-005-02).\n- `attachment`: a `multipart/form-data` request body to a notes create or update endpoint (US-005-02, `ASM-BE-217`).\n- `real-time`: a WebSocket upgrade request, and an SSE connection request (US-005-03).\n- `real-time`: the path `/ws` (US-005-05).\n\nThe `mobile` category has no supplied matcher, and neither does polling-push (`GAP-BE-228`, `GAP-BE-233`).\n\nNothing from a refused request is stored. This module's logs must follow `backend.observability` and `ASM-BE-012`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guard.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guard.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## State machine\n\n`scope-guard` owns no entity with a status column. It has a per-request classification flow, derived from US-005-02 to US-005-04.
